\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\exists v,x^{\prime},\mathit{vs}^{\prime}\ldotp x\mapsto v,x^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}v\mathbin{\in}\mathbb{N}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list_{\mathbb{N}}}(x^{\prime},\mathit{vs}^{\prime})\mathrel{\star}\mathit{vs}=v:\mathit{vs}^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\\
\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{v}=v\mathrel{\star}\alpha=3|\mathit{vs}|+2\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{r}=\mathtt{null}\end{array}~\right)}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\exists v,\mathit{vs}^{\prime}\ldotp\mathsf{list_{\mathbb{N}}}(x,v:\mathit{vs}^{\prime})\mathrel{\star}\mathit{vs}=v:\mathit{vs}^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{v}=v\mathrel{\star}\alpha=3|\mathit{vs}|+2\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{r}=\mathtt{null}\end{array}~)}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\begin{array}[]{@{}m{10pt}|l@{}}\text{\rotatebox{90.0}{\ { exists, equiv }\ }&\begin{array}[]{@{}l@{}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{list_{\mathbb{N}}}(x,v:\mathit{vs}^{\prime})\mathrel{\star}\mathit{vs}=v:\mathit{vs}^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{v}=v\mathrel{\star}{v\mathbin{\in}\mathbb{N}}\mathrel{\star}\alpha=3|\mathit{vs}|+2\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{r}=\mathtt{null}\end{array}~)}\\
\mathtt{if}\ (\mathsf{even}(\mathsf{v}))\ \{\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{list_{\mathbb{N}}}(x,v:\mathit{vs}^{\prime})\mathrel{\star}\mathit{vs}=v:\mathit{vs}^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{v}=v\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{even}(v)\\
\mathrel{\star}\alpha-1=3|v:\mathit{vs}^{\prime}|+1-(v\bmod 2)\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{r}=\mathtt{null}\end{array}~\right)}\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\text{{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}// as $\alpha-1<\alpha$, we can apply $g$'s specification for $\alpha-1$}}\\
\begin{array}[]{@{}m{10pt}|l@{}}\text{\rotatebox{90.0}{\ {fr, eq}\ }&\begin{array}[]{@{}l@{}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{r}=\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list_{\mathbb{N}}}(x,v:\mathit{vs}^{\prime})\mathrel{\star}\alpha-1=3|v:\mathit{vs}^{\prime}|+1-(v\bmod 2)\end{array}~)}\\
\mathsf{r}\mathtt{\ :=\ }\mathtt{g}(\mathsf{x});\\
{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list_{\mathbb{N}}}(x,v:\mathit{vs}^{\prime})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{r}=|v:\mathit{vs}^{\prime}|\mathrel{\star}\alpha-1=3|v:\mathit{vs}^{\prime}|+1-(v\bmod 2)\end{array}~)}\end{array}\end{array}\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{list_{\mathbb{N}}}(x,v:\mathit{vs}^{\prime})\mathrel{\star}\mathit{vs}=v:\mathit{vs}^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{v}=v\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{even}(v)\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{r}=|\mathit{vs}|\mathrel{\star}\alpha=3|\mathit{vs}|+2\end{array}~)}\\
\}\ \mathtt{else}\ \{\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{list_{\mathbb{N}}}(x,v:\mathit{vs}^{\prime})\mathrel{\star}\mathit{vs}=v:\mathit{vs}^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{v}=v\mathrel{\star}\\
\mathsf{odd}(v)\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{r}=\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\alpha-1=3|\mathit{vs}|+(v\bmod 2)\end{array}~\right)}\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\text{{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}// as $\alpha-1<\alpha$, we can apply $f$'s specification for $\alpha-1$ }}\\
\begin{array}[]{@{}m{10pt}|l@{}}\text{\rotatebox{90.0}{\ {fr, eq}\ }&\begin{array}[]{@{}l@{}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{list_{\mathbb{N}}}(x,v:\mathit{vs}^{\prime})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{r}=\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\alpha-1=3|\mathit{vs}|+(v\bmod 2)\end{array}~)}\\
\mathsf{r}\mathtt{\ :=\ }\mathtt{f}(\mathsf{x});\\
{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{list_{\mathbb{N}}}(x,v:\mathit{vs}^{\prime})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{r}=|v:\mathit{vs}^{\prime}|\mathrel{\star}\alpha-1=3|\mathit{vs}|+(v\bmod 2)\end{array}~)}\end{array}\end{array}\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{list_{\mathbb{N}}}(x,v:\mathit{vs}^{\prime})\mathrel{\star}\mathit{vs}=v:\mathit{vs}^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{v}=v\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{odd}(v)\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{r}=|\mathit{vs}|\mathrel{\star}\alpha=3|\mathit{vs}|+2\end{array}~)}\\
\}\\
{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{list_{\mathbb{N}}}(x,v:\mathit{vs}^{\prime})\mathrel{\star}\mathit{vs}=v:\mathit{vs}^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{v}=v\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{r}=|\mathit{vs}|\mathrel{\star}(\mathsf{even}(v)\vee\mathsf{odd}(v))\mathrel{\star}\\
\alpha=3|\mathit{vs}|+2\end{array}~\right)}\end{array}\end{array}}}}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\exists v\in\mathbb{N},\mathit{vs}^{\prime}\ldotp\mathsf{list_{\mathbb{N}}}(x,v:\mathit{vs}^{\prime})\mathrel{\star}\mathit{vs}=v:\mathit{vs}^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{v}=v\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{r}=|\mathit{vs}|\mathrel{\star}\alpha=3|\mathit{vs}|+2\end{array}~)}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}(x=\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\mathit{vs}=\epsilon\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{r}=|\mathit{vs}|\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{v}=\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\alpha=3|\mathit{vs}|+2)~\vee\\
(\exists v\in\mathbb{N},\mathit{vs}^{\prime}\ldotp\mathsf{list_{\mathbb{N}}}(x,v:\mathit{vs}^{\prime})\mathrel{\star}\mathit{vs}=v:\mathit{vs}^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{v}=v\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{r}=|\mathit{vs}|\mathrel{\star}\alpha=3|\mathit{vs}|+2)\end{array}~\right)}

\displaystyle\Gamma(\alpha)\vdash

\displaystyle~{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{list_{\mathbb{N}}}(x,v:vs^{\prime})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\alpha=3|v:vs^{\prime}|+(v\bmod 2)\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{v},\mathsf{r}=\mathtt{null}\end{array}~)}

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\exists x^{\prime}\ldotp x\mapsto v,x^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}{v\mathbin{\in}\mathbb{N}}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list_{\mathbb{N}}}(x^{\prime},vs^{\prime})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\alpha=3|v:vs^{\prime}|+(v\bmod 2)\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{v},\mathsf{r}=\mathtt{null}\end{array}~)}

\displaystyle\mathsf{v}\mathtt{\ :=\ }[\mathsf{x}];

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\exists x^{\prime}\ldotp x\mapsto v,x^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}{v\mathbin{\in}\mathbb{N}}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list_{\mathbb{N}}}(x^{\prime},vs^{\prime})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{v}=v\mathrel{\star}\alpha=3|v:vs^{\prime}|+(v\bmod 2)\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{r}=\mathtt{null}\end{array}~)}

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\exists x^{\prime}\ldotp x\mapsto v,x^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list_{\mathbb{N}}}(x^{\prime},vs^{\prime})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{v}=v\mathrel{\star}{v\mathbin{\in}\mathbb{N}}\mathrel{\star}\alpha=3|v:vs^{\prime}|+(v\bmod 2)\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{r}=\mathtt{null}\end{array}~)}

\displaystyle\mathtt{if}\ (\mathsf{even}(\mathsf{v}))\ \{

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\exists x^{\prime}\ldotp x\mapsto v,x^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list_{\mathbb{N}}}(x^{\prime},vs^{\prime})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{v}=v\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{even}(v)\mathrel{\star}\alpha=3|v:vs^{\prime}|\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{r}=\mathtt{null}\end{array}~)}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\mathsf{x}\mathtt{\ :=\ }[\mathsf{x}+1];

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\exists x^{\prime}\ldotp x\mapsto v,x^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list_{\mathbb{N}}}(x^{\prime},vs^{\prime})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{x}=x^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{v}=v\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{even}(v)\mathrel{\star}\alpha-1=3|vs^{\prime}|+2\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{r}=\mathtt{null}\end{array}~)}

// as
3\alpha-1<\alpha
, we can apply LL’s specifications for
\alpha-1

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\begin{array}[]{@{}m{10pt}|l@{}}\text{\rotatebox{90.0}{\ {fr + ex}\ }&\begin{array}[]{@{}l@{}}\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{list_{\mathbb{N}}}(x^{\prime},vs^{\prime})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{x}=x^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{r}=\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\alpha-1=3|vs^{\prime}|+2\end{array}~)}\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\mathsf{r}\mathtt{\ :=\ }\mathtt{LL}(\mathsf{x});\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{list_{\mathbb{N}}}(x^{\prime},vs^{\prime})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{x}=x^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{r}=|vs^{\prime}|\mathrel{\star}\alpha-1=3|vs^{\prime}|+2\end{array}~)}\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\mathsf{r}\mathtt{\ :=\ }\mathsf{r}+1;\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{list_{\mathbb{N}}}(x^{\prime},vs^{\prime})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{x}=x^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{r}=|vs^{\prime}|+1\mathrel{\star}\alpha-1=3|vs^{\prime}|+2\end{array}~)}\end{array}\end{array}}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\exists x^{\prime}\ldotp x\mapsto v,x^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list_{\mathbb{N}}}(x^{\prime},vs^{\prime})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{x}=x^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{v}=v\mathrel{\star}\\
\mathsf{r}=|v:\mathit{vs}^{\prime}|\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{even}(v)\mathrel{\star}\alpha=3|v:vs^{\prime}|+(v\bmod 2)\end{array}~\right)}

\displaystyle\}\ \mathtt{else}\ \{

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\exists x^{\prime}\ldotp x\mapsto v,x^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list_{\mathbb{N}}}(x^{\prime},vs^{\prime})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{v}=v\mathrel{\star}\\
\mathsf{odd}(\mathsf{v})\mathrel{\star}\alpha-1=3|v:vs^{\prime}|+1-(v\bmod 2)\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{r}=\mathtt{null}\end{array}~\right)}

// as
\alpha-1<\alpha
, we can apply
g
’s specification for
\alpha-1

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\begin{array}[]{@{}m{10pt}|l@{}}\text{\rotatebox{90.0}{\ {fr + ex}\ }&\begin{array}[]{@{}l@{}}\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{list_{\mathbb{N}}}(x,v:vs^{\prime})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{r}=\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\alpha-1=3|v:vs^{\prime}|+1-(v\bmod 2)\end{array}~)}\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\mathsf{r}\mathtt{\ :=\ }\mathtt{g}(\mathsf{x});\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{list_{\mathbb{N}}}(x,v:vs^{\prime})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{r}=|v:vs^{\prime}|\mathrel{\star}\alpha-1=3|v:vs^{\prime}|+1-(v\bmod 2)\end{array}~)}\end{array}\end{array}}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\exists x^{\prime}\ldotp x\mapsto v,x^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list_{\mathbb{N}}}(x^{\prime},vs^{\prime})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{v}=v\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{odd}(\mathsf{v})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{r}=|v:\mathit{vs}|\mathrel{\star}\\
\alpha=3|v:vs^{\prime}|+(v\bmod 2)\end{array}~\right)}

\displaystyle\}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\exists x^{\prime}\ldotp x\mapsto v,x^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list_{\mathbb{N}}}(x^{\prime},vs^{\prime})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{v}=v\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{r}=|v:\mathit{vs}^{\prime}|\mathrel{\star}\\
(\mathsf{x}=x^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{even}(v)\vee\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{odd}(v))\mathrel{\star}\alpha=3|v:vs^{\prime}|+(v\bmod 2)\end{array}~\right)}

To conclude the proof, we need to show that
Q^{\prime}_{LL}(\alpha)
,
Q^{\prime}_{f}(\alpha)
and
Q^{\prime}_{g}(\alpha)
are in the internalisations of their external counterparts, which is done as follows, again eliding the proof for
Q_{g}(\alpha)
as it is analogous to that of
Q_{f}(\alpha)
.

\begin{array}[]{l l}&\exists\vec{p}\ldotp Q^{\prime}_{LL}(\alpha)[\vec{p}/\vec{\mathsf{p}}]\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{ret}=\mathsf{r}[\vec{p}/\vec{\mathsf{p}}]\\
\Leftrightarrow&\exists p_{x},p_{r},p_{v}\ldotp\big((x=\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\mathit{vs}=\epsilon\mathrel{\star}p_{x}=x\mathrel{\star}p_{r}=|\mathit{vs}|\mathrel{\star}p_{v}=\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\alpha=3|\mathit{vs}|+2)~\lor\\
&\quad\quad(\exists v,\mathit{vs}^{\prime}\ldotp\mathsf{list_{\mathbb{N}}}(x,v:\mathit{vs}^{\prime})\mathrel{\star}\mathit{vs}=v:\mathit{vs}^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}p_{x}=x\mathrel{\star}p_{v}=v\mathrel{\star}p_{r}=|\mathit{vs}|\\
&\quad\quad\quad\quad\mathrel{\star}\alpha=3|\mathit{vs}|+2)\big)\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{ret}=p_{r}\\
\Leftrightarrow&\mathsf{list_{\mathbb{N}}}(x,\mathit{vs})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{ret}=|\mathit{vs}|\mathrel{\star}\alpha=3|\mathit{vs}|+2\\
\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{l l}&\exists\vec{p}\ldotp Q^{\prime}_{f}(\alpha)[\vec{p}/\vec{\mathsf{p}}]\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{ret}=\mathsf{r}[\vec{p}/\vec{\mathsf{p}}]\\
\Leftrightarrow&\exists p_{x},p_{v},p_{r},x^{\prime}\ldotp x\mapsto v,x^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list_{\mathbb{N}}}(x^{\prime},\mathit{vs}^{\prime})\mathrel{\star}p_{v}=v\mathrel{\star}p_{r}=|v:\mathit{vs}^{\prime}|\\
&\quad\quad\mathrel{\star}(p_{x}=x^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{even}(v)\vee p_{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{odd}(v))\mathrel{\star}\alpha=3|v:\mathit{vs}^{\prime}|+(v\bmod 2)\\
\Leftrightarrow&\exists x^{\prime}\ldotp x\mapsto v,x^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list_{\mathbb{N}}}(x^{\prime},\mathit{vs}^{\prime})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{ret}=|v:\mathit{vs}^{\prime}|\\
&\quad\quad\mathrel{\star}(\mathsf{even}(v)\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{odd}(v))\mathrel{\star}\alpha=3|v:\mathit{vs}^{\prime}|+(v\bmod 2)\\
\Leftrightarrow&\exists x^{\prime}\ldotp x\mapsto v,x^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list_{\mathbb{N}}}(x^{\prime},\mathit{vs}^{\prime})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{ret}=|v:\mathit{vs}^{\prime}|\mathrel{\star}{v\mathbin{\in}\mathbb{N}}\mathrel{\star}\alpha=3|v:\mathit{vs}^{\prime}|+(v\bmod 2)\\
\Leftrightarrow&\mathsf{list_{\mathbb{N}}}(x,v:\mathit{vs}^{\prime})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{ret}=|v:\mathit{vs}^{\prime}|\mathrel{\star}\alpha=3|v:\mathit{vs}^{\prime}|+(v\bmod 2)\end{array}

F.3
List Swap

We now consider a list-swap function that swaps the two first elements of a given list; if the given list is too short, an error is triggered.

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(x,n)\end{array}~)}

\displaystyle\mathtt{LSwapFirstTwo}(\mathsf{x})~\{

\displaystyle\quad\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathit{ok}:\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(x,n)\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{a},\mathsf{b},\mathsf{y}=\mathtt{null}\end{array}~)}

\displaystyle\quad\!\!\mathtt{if}\ (\mathsf{x}=\mathtt{null})\ \{

\displaystyle\quad\!\!\quad\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathit{ok}:\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(x,n)\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{a},\mathsf{b},\mathsf{x},\mathsf{y}=\mathtt{null}\end{array}~)}

\displaystyle\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\mathtt{error}{(\mathtt{``List~too~short!"})}

\displaystyle\quad\!\!\quad\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathit{err}:\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(x,n)\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{a},\mathsf{b},\mathsf{x},\mathsf{y}=\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{err}=\mathtt{``List~too~short!"}\end{array}~)}

\displaystyle\quad\!\!\}\ \mathtt{else}\ \{

\displaystyle\quad\!\!\quad\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathit{ok}:\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(x,n)\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{a},\mathsf{b},\mathsf{y}=\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{x}\mathbin{\neq}\mathtt{null}\end{array}~)}

\displaystyle\quad\!\!\quad\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathit{ok}:\exists v,x^{\prime}\ldotp\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}x\mapsto v,x^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(x^{\prime},n-1)\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{a},\mathsf{b},\mathsf{y}=\mathtt{null}\end{array}~)}

\displaystyle\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\mathsf{y}\mathtt{\ :=\ }[\mathsf{x}+1];

\displaystyle\quad\!\!\quad\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathit{ok}:\exists v\ldotp\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}x\mapsto v,\mathsf{y}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(\mathsf{y},n-1)\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{a},\mathsf{b}=\mathtt{null}\end{array}~)}

\displaystyle\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\mathtt{if}\ (\mathsf{y}=\mathtt{null})\ \{

\displaystyle\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\quad\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathit{ok}:\exists v\ldotp\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}x\mapsto v,\mathsf{y}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(\mathsf{y},n-1)\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{a},\mathsf{b}=\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{y}=\mathtt{null}\end{array}~)}

\displaystyle\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\mathtt{error}{(\mathtt{``List~too~short!"})}

\displaystyle\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\quad\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathit{err}:\exists v\ldotp\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}x\mapsto v,\mathsf{y}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(\mathsf{y},n-1)\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{a},\mathsf{b}=\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{y}=\mathtt{null}\\
\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{err}=\mathtt{``List~too~short!"}\end{array}~\right)}

\displaystyle\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\}\ \mathtt{else}\ \{

\displaystyle\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\quad\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathit{ok}:\exists v\ldotp\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}x\mapsto v,\mathsf{y}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(\mathsf{y},n-1)\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{a},\mathsf{b}=\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{y}\mathbin{\neq}\mathtt{null}\end{array}~)}

\displaystyle\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\quad\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathit{ok}:\exists v,v^{\prime},x^{\prime}\ldotp\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}x\mapsto v,\mathsf{y}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{y}\mapsto v^{\prime},x^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(x^{\prime},n-2)\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{a},\mathsf{b}=\mathtt{null}\end{array}~\right)}

\displaystyle\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\mathsf{a}\mathtt{\ :=\ }[\mathsf{x}];

\displaystyle\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\quad\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathit{ok}:\exists v,v^{\prime},x^{\prime}\ldotp\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}x\mapsto v,\mathsf{y}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{y}\mapsto v^{\prime},x^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(x^{\prime},n-2)\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{a}=v\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{b}=\mathtt{null}\end{array}~\right)}

\displaystyle\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\mathsf{b}\mathtt{\ :=\ }[\mathsf{y}];

\displaystyle\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\quad\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathit{ok}:\exists v,v^{\prime},x^{\prime}\ldotp\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}x\mapsto v,\mathsf{y}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{y}\mapsto v^{\prime},x^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(x^{\prime},n-2)\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{a}=v\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{b}=v^{\prime}\end{array}~\right)}

\displaystyle\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\quad\!\![\mathsf{y}]\mathtt{\ :=\ }\mathsf{a};

\displaystyle\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\quad\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathit{ok}:\exists v,v^{\prime},x^{\prime}\ldotp\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}x\mapsto v,\mathsf{y}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{y}\mapsto v,x^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(x^{\prime},n-2)\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{a}=v\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{b}=v^{\prime}\end{array}~\right)}

\displaystyle\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\quad\!\![\mathsf{x}]\mathtt{\ :=\ }\mathsf{b}

\displaystyle\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\quad\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathit{ok}:\exists v,v^{\prime},x^{\prime}\ldotp\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}x\mapsto v^{\prime},\mathsf{y}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{y}\mapsto v,x^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(x^{\prime},n-2)\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{a}=v\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{b}=v^{\prime}\end{array}~\right)}

\displaystyle\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\quad\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathit{ok}:\exists x^{\prime}\ldotp\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}x\mapsto\mathsf{b},\mathsf{y}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{y}\mapsto\mathsf{a},x^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(x^{\prime},n-2)\end{array}~\right)}

\displaystyle\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\}

\displaystyle\quad\!\!\quad\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathit{ok}:\exists x^{\prime}\ldotp\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}x\mapsto\mathsf{b},\mathsf{y}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{y}\mapsto\mathsf{a},x^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(x^{\prime},n-2)\\
\mathit{err}:\exists v\ldotp\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}x\mapsto v,\mathsf{y}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(\mathsf{y},n-1)\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{a},\mathsf{b}=\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{y}=\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\\
\hskip 28.45274pt\mathsf{err}=\mathtt{``List~too~short!"}\end{array}~\right)}

\displaystyle\quad\!\!\};

\displaystyle\quad\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathit{ok}:\exists x^{\prime}\ldotp\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}x\mapsto\mathsf{b},\mathsf{y}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{y}\mapsto\mathsf{a},x^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(x^{\prime},n-2)\\
\mathit{err}:(\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(x,n)\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{a},\mathsf{b},\mathsf{x},\mathsf{y}=\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{err}=\mathtt{``List~too~short!"})~\lor\\
\hskip 28.45274pt(\exists v\ldotp\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}x\mapsto v,\mathsf{y}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(\mathsf{y},n-1)\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{a},\mathsf{b}=\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{y}=\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\\
\hskip 28.45274pt\mathsf{err}=\mathtt{``List~too~short!"})\end{array}~\right)}

\displaystyle\quad\!\!\mathtt{return}\ \mathsf{\mathtt{null}}

\displaystyle\}

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathit{ok}:\mathsf{list}(x,n)\mathrel{\star}n\geq 2\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{ret}=\mathtt{null}\\
\mathit{err}:\mathsf{list}(x,n)\mathrel{\star}n<2\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{err}=\mathtt{``List~too~short!"}\end{array}~\right)}

F.4
List Insert First

We consider a list algorithm that inserts a new element
\mathsf{v}
at the beginning of a given list
\mathsf{x}
:

\begin{array}[]{l}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{v}=v\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(x,\mathit{xs},\mathit{vs})\end{array}~)}\\
\mathtt{LInsertFirst}(\mathsf{x},\mathsf{v})~\{\\
\quad\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{v}=v\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(x,\mathit{xs},\mathit{vs})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{y}=\mathtt{null}\end{array}~)}\\
\quad\!\!\mathsf{y}\mathtt{\ :=\ }\mathtt{new}(2);\\
\quad\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{v}=v\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(x,\mathit{xs},\mathit{vs})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{y}\mapsto\mathtt{null},\mathtt{null}\end{array}~)}\\
\quad\!\![\mathsf{y}]\mathtt{\ :=\ }v;\\
\quad\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{v}=v\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(x,\mathit{xs},\mathit{vs})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{y}\mapsto v,\mathtt{null}\end{array}~)}\\
\quad\!\![\mathsf{y}+1]\mathtt{\ :=\ }x;\\
\quad\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{v}=v\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(x,\mathit{xs},\mathit{vs})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{y}\mapsto v,x\end{array}~)}\\
\quad\!\!\mathtt{return}\ \mathsf{y}\\
\}\\
{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{list}(x,\mathit{xs},\mathit{vs})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{ret}\mapsto v,x\end{array}~)}\\
{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{list}(\mathsf{ret},\mathsf{ret}:\mathit{xs},v:\mathit{vs})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{listHead}(x,\mathit{xs})\end{array}~)}\end{array}

F.5
List Insert Last

We consider an algorithm for inserting a new element
\mathsf{v}
at the end of a given list
\mathsf{x}
:

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{v}=v\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(x,\mathit{xs},\mathit{vs})\end{array}~)}

\displaystyle\mathtt{LInsertLast}(\mathsf{x},\mathsf{v})~\{

\displaystyle\quad\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{v}=v\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(x,\mathit{xs},\mathit{vs})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{n},\mathsf{p},\mathsf{y}=\mathtt{null}\end{array}~)}

\displaystyle\quad\!\!\mathsf{y}\mathtt{\ :=\ }\mathtt{new}(2);

\displaystyle\quad\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{v}=v\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(x,\mathit{xs},\mathit{vs})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{y}\mapsto\mathtt{null},\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{n},\mathsf{p}=\mathtt{null}\end{array}~)}

\displaystyle\quad\!\![\mathsf{y}]\mathtt{\ :=\ }\mathsf{v};

\displaystyle\quad\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{v}=v\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(x,\mathit{xs},\mathit{vs})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{y}\mapsto v,\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{n},\mathsf{p}=\mathtt{null}\end{array}~)}

\displaystyle\quad\!\!\mathtt{if}\ (\mathsf{x}=\mathtt{null})\ \{

\displaystyle\quad\!\!\quad\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{v}=v\mathrel{\star}\mathit{xs},\mathit{vs}=[~]\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{y}\mapsto v,\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{n},\mathsf{p},\mathsf{x}=\mathtt{null}\end{array}~)}

\displaystyle\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\mathsf{x}\mathtt{\ :=\ }\mathsf{y}

\displaystyle\quad\!\!\quad\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{x}=\mathsf{y}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{v}=v\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(\mathsf{x},\mathit{xs}\mathbin{\cdot}[\mathsf{x}],\mathit{vs}\mathbin{\cdot}[v])\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{listHead}(x,\mathit{xs})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{n},\mathsf{p},x=\mathtt{null}\end{array}~)}

\displaystyle\quad\!\!\}\ \mathtt{else}\ \{

\displaystyle\quad\!\!\quad\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{v}=v\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(x,\mathit{xs},\mathit{vs})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{y}\mapsto v,\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{n},\mathsf{p}=\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{x}\mathbin{\neq}\mathtt{null}\end{array}~)}

\displaystyle\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\mathsf{p}\mathtt{\ :=\ }\mathsf{x};

\displaystyle\quad\!\!\quad\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{v}=v\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(x,\mathit{xs},\mathit{vs})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{y}\mapsto v,\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{n}=\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{p}=\mathsf{x}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{x}\mathbin{\neq}\mathtt{null}\end{array}~)}

\displaystyle\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\mathsf{n}\mathtt{\ :=\ }[\mathsf{x}+1];

\displaystyle\quad\!\!\quad\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}P_{0}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{v}=v\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{y}\mapsto v,\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{x}\mathbin{\neq}\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{listHead}(x,\mathit{xs})\end{array}~)}

\displaystyle\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\mathtt{while}\ (\mathsf{n}~\texttt{!=}~\mathtt{null})\ \{

\displaystyle\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\quad\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}P_{i}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{n}\mathbin{\neq}\mathtt{null}\end{array}~)}

\displaystyle\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\mathsf{p}\mathtt{\ :=\ }\mathsf{n};

\displaystyle\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\quad\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\exists\mathit{xs}_{\alpha},\mathit{xs}_{\beta},\mathit{vs}_{\alpha},v^{\prime},\mathit{vs}_{\beta},p\ldotp\mathsf{lseg}(x,p,\mathit{xs}_{\alpha},\mathit{vs}_{\alpha})\mathrel{\star}p\mapsto v^{\prime},\mathsf{n}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(\mathsf{n},\mathit{xs}_{\beta},\mathit{vs}_{\beta})\mathrel{\star}\\
\mathit{xs}=\mathit{xs}_{\alpha}\mathbin{\cdot}[p]\mathbin{\cdot}\mathit{xs}_{\beta}\mathrel{\star}\mathit{vs}=\mathit{vs}_{\alpha}\mathbin{\cdot}[v^{\prime}]\mathbin{\cdot}\mathit{vs}_{\beta}\mathrel{\star}|\mathit{xs}_{\alpha}|=i\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{n}\mathbin{\neq}\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{p}=\mathsf{n}\end{array}~\right)}

\displaystyle\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\mathsf{n}\mathtt{\ :=\ }[\mathsf{n}+1]

\displaystyle\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\quad\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\exists\mathit{xs}_{\alpha},\mathit{xs}_{\beta},\mathit{vs}_{\alpha},v^{\prime},v^{\prime\prime},\mathit{vs}_{\beta},p\ldotp\mathsf{lseg}(x,p,\mathit{xs}_{\alpha},\mathit{vs}_{\alpha})\mathrel{\star}p\mapsto v^{\prime},\mathsf{p}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{p}\mapsto v^{\prime\prime},\mathsf{n}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(\mathsf{n},\mathit{xs}_{\beta},\mathit{vs}_{\beta})\mathrel{\star}\\
\mathit{xs}=\mathit{xs}_{\alpha}\mathbin{\cdot}[p,\mathsf{p}]\mathbin{\cdot}\mathit{xs}_{\beta}\mathrel{\star}\mathit{vs}=\mathit{vs}_{\alpha}\mathbin{\cdot}[v^{\prime},v^{\prime\prime}]\mathbin{\cdot}\mathit{vs}_{\beta}\mathrel{\star}|\mathit{xs}_{\alpha}|=i\end{array}~\right)}

\displaystyle\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\quad\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\exists\mathit{xs}_{\alpha},\mathit{xs}_{\beta},\mathit{vs}_{\alpha},v^{\prime\prime},\mathit{vs}_{\beta}\ldotp\mathsf{lseg}(x,\mathsf{p},\mathit{xs}_{\alpha}\mathbin{\cdot}[p],\mathit{vs}_{\alpha}\mathbin{\cdot}[v^{\prime}])\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{p}\mapsto v^{\prime\prime},\mathsf{n}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(\mathsf{n},\mathit{xs}_{\beta},\mathit{vs}_{\beta})\mathrel{\star}\\
\mathit{xs}=(\mathit{xs}_{\alpha}\mathbin{\cdot}[p])\mathbin{\cdot}[\mathsf{p}]\mathbin{\cdot}\mathit{xs}_{\beta}\mathrel{\star}\mathit{vs}=(\mathit{vs}_{\alpha}\mathbin{\cdot}[v^{\prime}])\mathbin{\cdot}[v^{\prime\prime}]\mathbin{\cdot}\mathit{vs}_{\beta}\mathrel{\star}|\mathit{xs}_{\alpha}|=i\end{array}~\right)}

\displaystyle\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\quad\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}P_{i+1}\end{array}~)}

\displaystyle\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\};

\displaystyle\quad\!\!\quad\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\exists m\ldotp P_{m}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{n}=\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{v}=v\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{y}\mapsto v,\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{x}\mathbin{\neq}\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{listHead}(x,\mathit{xs})\end{array}~)}

\displaystyle\quad\!\!\quad\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\exists v^{\prime}\ldotp\mathsf{lseg}(x,\mathsf{p},\mathit{xs}^{\prime},\mathit{vs}^{\prime})\mathrel{\star}\mathit{xs}=\mathit{xs}^{\prime}\mathbin{\cdot}[\mathsf{p}]\mathrel{\star}\mathit{vs}=\mathit{vs}^{\prime}\mathbin{\cdot}[v^{\prime}]\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{p}\mapsto v^{\prime},\mathsf{n}\mathrel{\star}\\
\mathsf{n}=\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{v}=v\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{y}\mapsto v,\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{x}\mathbin{\neq}\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{listHead}(x,\mathit{xs})\end{array}~\right)}

\displaystyle\quad\!\!\quad\!\![\mathsf{p}+1]\mathtt{\ :=\ }\mathsf{y}

\displaystyle\quad\!\!\quad\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{list}(\mathsf{x},\mathit{xs}\mathbin{\cdot}[\mathsf{y}],\mathit{vs}\mathbin{\cdot}[v])\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{n}=\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{v}=v\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{x}\mathbin{\neq}\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{listHead}(x,\mathit{xs})\end{array}~\right)}

\displaystyle\quad\!\!\};

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}(\mathsf{x}=\mathsf{y}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{v}=v\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(\mathsf{x},\mathit{xs}\mathbin{\cdot}[\mathsf{x}],\mathit{vs}\mathbin{\cdot}[v])\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{listHead}(x,\mathit{xs})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{n},\mathsf{p},x=\mathtt{null})~\lor\\
(\mathsf{list}(\mathsf{x},\mathit{xs}\mathbin{\cdot}[\mathsf{y}],\mathit{vs}\mathbin{\cdot}[v])\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{n}=\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{v}=v\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{x}\mathbin{\neq}\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{listHead}(x,\mathit{xs}))\end{array}~\right)}

\displaystyle\quad\!\!\mathtt{return}\ \mathsf{x}

\displaystyle\}

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\exists x^{\prime}\ldotp\mathsf{list}(\mathsf{ret},\mathit{xs}\mathbin{\cdot}[x^{\prime}],\mathit{vs}\mathbin{\cdot}[v])\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{listHead}(x,\mathit{xs})\end{array}~)}

where
P_{i}
denotes the loop invariant
\exists\mathit{xs}_{\alpha},\mathit{xs}_{\beta},\mathit{vs}_{\alpha},v^{\prime},\mathit{vs}_{\beta}\ldotp\mathsf{lseg}(x,\mathsf{p},\mathit{xs}_{\alpha},\mathit{vs}_{\alpha})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{p}\mapsto v^{\prime},\mathsf{n}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(\mathsf{n},\mathit{xs}_{\beta},\mathit{vs}_{\beta})\mathrel{\star}\mathit{xs}=\mathit{xs}_{\alpha}\mathbin{\cdot}[\mathsf{p}]\mathbin{\cdot}\mathit{xs}_{\beta}\mathrel{\star}\mathit{vs}=\mathit{vs}_{\alpha}\mathbin{\cdot}[v^{\prime}]\mathbin{\cdot}\mathit{vs}_{\beta}\mathrel{\star}|\mathit{xs}_{\alpha}|=i
.

F.6
List Free

We consider the list-free algorithm,
\mathtt{LFree}(\mathsf{x})
, which frees all the nodes of a given singly-linked list starting at
\mathsf{x}
. The algorithm is implemented as follows:

\begin{array}[]{l}\mathtt{LFree}(\mathsf{x})~\{\\
\quad\!\!\mathtt{if}\ (\mathsf{x}=\mathtt{null})\ \{\\
\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\mathtt{skip}\\
\quad\!\!\}\ \mathtt{else}\ \{\\
\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\mathsf{y}\mathtt{\ :=\ }\mathsf{x};\mathsf{x}\mathtt{\ :=\ }[\mathsf{x}+1];\\
\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\mathtt{free}(\mathsf{y});\mathtt{free}(\mathsf{y}+1);\\
\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\mathsf{r}\mathtt{\ :=\ }\mathtt{LFree}(\mathsf{x})\\
\quad\!\!\};\\
\quad\!\!\mathtt{return}\ \mathtt{null}\\
\}\\
\end{array}

The proof sketch of the body of the algorithm is given below. The measure we use to handle recursion is the length of the list, which corresponds to the number of pointers,
\alpha\triangleq|\mathit{xs}|
.

\small\begin{array}[]{r@{~}l}\Gamma(\alpha)\vdash&{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(x,\mathit{xs})\mathrel{\star}\alpha=|\mathit{xs}|\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{r},\mathsf{y}=\mathtt{null}\end{array}~)}\\
&\mathtt{if}\ (\mathsf{x}=\mathtt{null})\ \{\\
&\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}x=\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(x,\mathit{xs})\mathrel{\star}\alpha=|\mathit{xs}|\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{r},\mathsf{y}=\mathtt{null}\end{array}~)}\\
&\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\mathtt{skip}\\
&\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}x=\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(x,\mathit{xs})\mathrel{\star}\alpha=|\mathit{xs}|\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{r},\mathsf{y}=\mathtt{null}\end{array}~)}\\
&\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}x=\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\mathit{xs}=\epsilon\mathrel{\star}\alpha=|\mathit{xs}|\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{r},\mathsf{y}=\mathtt{null}\end{array}~)}\\
&\}\ \mathtt{else}\ \{\\
&\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}x\mathbin{\neq}\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(x,\mathit{xs})\mathrel{\star}\alpha=|\mathit{xs}|\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{r},\mathsf{y}=\mathtt{null}\end{array}~)}\\
&\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\exists x^{\prime},v,\mathit{xs}^{\prime}\ldotp\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}x\mapsto v,x^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(x^{\prime},\mathit{xs}^{\prime})\mathrel{\star}\mathit{xs}=x:\mathit{xs}^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\alpha=|\mathit{xs}|\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{r},\mathsf{y}=\mathtt{null}\end{array}~)}\\
&\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\mathsf{y}\mathtt{\ :=\ }\mathsf{x};\\
&\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\exists x^{\prime},v,\mathit{xs}^{\prime}\ldotp\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{y}=x\mathrel{\star}x\mapsto v,x^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(x^{\prime},\mathit{xs}^{\prime})\mathrel{\star}\mathit{xs}=x:\mathit{xs}^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\alpha=|\mathit{xs}|\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{r}=\mathtt{null}\end{array}~)}\\
&\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\mathsf{x}\mathtt{\ :=\ }[\mathsf{x}+1];\\
&\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\exists x^{\prime},v,\mathit{xs}^{\prime}\ldotp\mathsf{x}=x^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{y}=x\mathrel{\star}x\mapsto v,x^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(x^{\prime},\mathit{xs}^{\prime})\mathrel{\star}\mathit{xs}=x:\mathit{xs}^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\alpha=|\mathit{xs}|\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{r}=\mathtt{null}\end{array}~)}\\
&\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\exists x^{\prime},v,\mathit{xs}^{\prime}\ldotp\mathsf{x}=x^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{y}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{y}\mapsto v,x^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(x^{\prime},\mathit{xs}^{\prime})\mathrel{\star}\mathit{xs}=x:\mathit{xs}^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\alpha=|\mathit{xs}|\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{r}=\mathtt{null}\end{array}~)}\\
&\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\mathtt{free}(\mathsf{y});\\
&\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\exists x^{\prime},\mathit{xs}^{\prime}\ldotp\mathsf{x}=x^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{y}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{y}\mapsto\varnothing,x^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(x^{\prime},\mathit{xs}^{\prime})\mathrel{\star}\mathit{xs}=x:\mathit{xs}^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\alpha-1=|\mathit{xs}^{\prime}|\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{r}=\mathtt{null}\end{array}~)}\\
&\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\mathtt{free}(\mathsf{y}+1);\\
&\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\text{{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}// as $\alpha-1<\alpha$, we can apply the specification for $\alpha-1$ }}\\
&\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\exists x^{\prime},\mathit{xs}^{\prime}\ldotp\mathsf{x}=x^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{y}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{y}\mapsto\varnothing,\varnothing\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(x^{\prime},\mathit{xs}^{\prime})\mathrel{\star}\mathit{xs}=x:\mathit{xs}^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\alpha-1=|\mathit{xs}^{\prime}|\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{r}=\mathtt{null}\end{array}~)}\\
&\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\mathsf{r}\mathtt{\ :=\ }\mathtt{LFree}(\mathsf{x})\\
&\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\exists x^{\prime},\mathit{xs}^{\prime}\ldotp\mathsf{x}=x^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{y}=x\mathrel{\star}x\mapsto\varnothing,\varnothing\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{freed}(\mathit{xs}^{\prime})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{listHead}(x^{\prime},\mathit{xs}^{\prime})\mathrel{\star}\mathit{xs}=x:\mathit{xs}^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\\
\alpha-1=|\mathit{xs}^{\prime}|\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{r}=\mathtt{null}\end{array}~\right)}\\
&\};\\
&{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}(\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}x=\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\mathit{xs}=\epsilon\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{r},\mathsf{y}=\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\alpha=|\mathit{xs}|)~\vee\\
(\exists x^{\prime},\mathit{xs}^{\prime}\ldotp\mathsf{x}=x^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{y}=x\mathrel{\star}x\mapsto\varnothing,\varnothing\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{freed}(\mathit{xs}^{\prime})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{listHead}(x^{\prime},\mathit{xs}^{\prime})\mathrel{\star}\mathit{xs}=x:\mathit{xs}^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\\
\mathsf{r}=\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\alpha=|\mathit{xs}|)\end{array}~\right)}\end{array}

We conclude the proof by moving from the internal to the external specification:

\begin{array}[]{r l}&\exists p_{x},p_{y},p_{r}.~\mathsf{ret}=\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}(p_{x}=x\mathrel{\star}x=\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\mathit{xs}=\epsilon\mathrel{\star}p_{r},p_{y}=\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\alpha=|\mathit{xs}|)~\vee\\
&\quad\quad(\exists x^{\prime},\mathit{xs}^{\prime}\ldotp p_{x}=x^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}p_{y}=x\mathrel{\star}x\mapsto\varnothing,\varnothing\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{freed}(\mathit{xs}^{\prime})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{listHead}(x^{\prime},\mathit{xs}^{\prime})\mathrel{\star}\mathit{xs}=x:\mathit{xs}^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\\
&\quad\quad p_{r}=\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\alpha=|\mathit{xs}|)\\
\Leftrightarrow&\mathsf{ret}=\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\alpha=|\mathit{xs}|\mathrel{\star}((x=\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\mathit{xs}=\epsilon)\lor(\exists\mathit{xs}^{\prime}\ldotp x\mapsto\varnothing,\varnothing\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{freed}(\mathit{xs}^{\prime})\mathrel{\star}\mathit{xs}=x:\mathit{xs}^{\prime}))\\
\Leftrightarrow&\mathsf{freed}(\mathit{xs})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{listHead}(x,\mathit{xs})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{ret}=\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\alpha=|\mathit{xs}|\end{array}

F.7
List Algorithm Client

We consider the following client of our proven-correct list algorithms

\begin{array}[]{l}\mathtt{LClient}(\mathsf{x})~\{\\
\quad\!\!\mathsf{l}\mathtt{\ :=\ }\mathtt{LLen(\mathsf{x})};\\
\quad\!\!\mathtt{if}\ (\mathsf{l}<5)\ \{\\
\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\mathsf{r}\mathtt{\ :=\ }\mathtt{LFree(\mathsf{x})};\\
\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\mathtt{error}(\mathtt{``List~too~short!"})\\
\quad\!\!\}\ \mathtt{else}\ \{\\
\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\mathtt{if}\ (\mathsf{l}>10)\ \{\\
\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\mathtt{while}\ ({\sf true})\ \{~\mathtt{skip}~\}\\
\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\}\ \mathtt{else}\ \{\\
\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\mathsf{r}\mathtt{\ :=\ }\mathtt{LPRev(\mathsf{l})}\\
\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\}\\
\quad\!\!\};\\
\quad\!\!\mathtt{return}\ \mathsf{r}\\
\}\\
\end{array}

and prove that it satisfies the following ESL specification:

\begin{array}[]{c}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(x,\mathit{xs})\end{array}~)}\\
{\mathtt{LClient}(\mathsf{x})}\\
{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathit{ok}:5\mathbin{\leq}|\mathit{xs}|\mathbin{\leq}10\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(\mathsf{ret},\mathit{xs}^{\dagger})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{listHead}(x,\mathit{xs})\end{array}~)}\\
{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathit{err}:|\mathit{xs}|\mathbin{<}5\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{freed}(\mathit{xs})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{listHead}(x,\mathit{xs})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{err}=\mathtt{``List~too~short!"}\end{array}~)}\end{array}

We prove the three branches of the client separately, exposing the non-terminating case, and then join the obtained specifications through the admissible disjunction property, to obtain the above specification. We give two of the three proofs for the branches below; the third is analogous to the first. We denote the client’s success post-condition by
Q_{\mathit{ok}}
, the client’s faulting post-condition by
Q_{\mathit{err}}
, and assume a specification context
\Gamma
that has the appropriate specifications of the called functions.

\displaystyle\Gamma\vdash

\displaystyle~{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(x,\mathit{xs})\mathrel{\star}|\mathit{xs}|\mathbin{<}5\end{array}~)}

\displaystyle\mathtt{LClient}(\mathsf{x})~\{

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(x,\mathit{xs})\mathrel{\star}|\mathit{xs}|\mathbin{<}5\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{l},\mathsf{r}=\mathtt{null}\end{array}~)}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\mathsf{l}\mathtt{\ :=\ }\mathtt{LLen(\mathsf{x})};

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}{\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(x,\mathit{xs})}\mathrel{\star}|\mathit{xs}|\mathbin{<}5\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{l}=|\mathit{xs}|\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{r}=\mathtt{null}\end{array}~\right)}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\mathtt{if}\ (\mathsf{l}<5)\ \{

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(x,\mathit{xs})\mathrel{\star}|\mathit{xs}|\mathbin{<}5\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{l}=|\mathit{xs}|\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{r}=\mathtt{null}\end{array}~\right)}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\mathsf{r}\mathtt{\ :=\ }\mathtt{LFree(\mathsf{x})};

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{freed}(\mathit{xs})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{listHead}(x,\mathit{xs})\mathrel{\star}|\mathit{xs}|\mathbin{<}5\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{l}=|\mathit{xs}|\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{r}=\mathtt{null}\end{array}~\right)}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\mathtt{error}(\mathtt{``LTS"})

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathit{err}:~Q_{\mathit{err}}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{l}=|\mathit{xs}|\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{r}=\mathtt{null}\end{array}~)}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\}\ \mathtt{else}\ \{

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{False}\end{array}~)}~\ldots~{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{False}\end{array}~)}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\};

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathit{err}:~Q_{\mathit{err}}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{l}=|\mathit{xs}|\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{r}=\mathtt{null}\end{array}~)}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\mathtt{return}\ \mathsf{r}

\displaystyle\}

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathit{err}:Q_{\mathit{err}}\mathrel{\star}\exists p_{x},p_{l},p_{r}.~p_{x}=x\mathrel{\star}p_{l}=|\mathit{xs}|\mathrel{\star}p_{r}=\mathtt{null}\end{array}~\right)}

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathit{err}:Q_{\mathit{err}}\end{array}~)}

\displaystyle\Gamma\vdash

\displaystyle~{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(x,\mathit{xs})\mathrel{\star}|\mathit{xs}|\mathbin{>}10\end{array}~)}

\displaystyle\mathtt{LClient}(\mathsf{x})~\{

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(x,\mathit{xs})\mathrel{\star}|\mathit{xs}|\mathbin{>}10\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{l},\mathsf{r}=\mathtt{null}\end{array}~\right)}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\mathsf{l}\mathtt{\ :=\ }\mathtt{LLen(\mathsf{x})};

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}{\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(x,\mathit{xs})}\mathrel{\star}|\mathit{xs}|\mathbin{>}10\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{l}=|\mathit{xs}|\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{r}=\mathtt{null}\end{array}~\right)}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\mathtt{if}\ (\mathsf{l}<5)\ \{

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{False}\end{array}~)}~\ldots~{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{False}\end{array}~)}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\}\ \mathtt{else}\ \{

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}{\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(x,\mathit{xs})}\mathrel{\star}|\mathit{xs}|\mathbin{>}10\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{l}=|\mathit{xs}|\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{r}=\mathtt{null}\end{array}~\right)}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\mathtt{if}\ (\mathsf{l}>10)\ \{

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}{\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(x,\mathit{xs})}\mathrel{\star}|\mathit{xs}|\mathbin{>}10\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{l}=|\mathit{xs}|\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{r}=\mathtt{null}\end{array}~\right)}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\mathtt{while}\ ({\sf true})\ \{~\mathtt{skip}~\}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{False}\end{array}~)}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\}\ \mathtt{else}\ \{

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{False}\end{array}~)}~\ldots~{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{False}\end{array}~)}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\};

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{False}\end{array}~)}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\mathtt{return}\ \mathsf{r}

\displaystyle\}

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{False}\end{array}~)}

The three obtained specifications then yield via disjunction:

\small{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left(\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}(\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(x,\mathit{xs})\mathrel{\star}|\mathit{xs}|\mathbin{<}5)~\lor\\
(\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(x,\mathit{xs})\mathrel{\star}5\mathbin{\leq}|\mathit{xs}|\mathbin{\leq}10)~\lor\\
(\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(x,\mathit{xs})\mathrel{\star}|\mathit{xs}|\mathbin{>}10)\end{array}\right)}\ {\mathtt{LClient}(\mathsf{x})}\ {\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left(\mathit{ok}:\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\mathsf{False}\lor Q_{\mathit{ok}}\lor\mathsf{False}\end{array}\right)}\ {\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left(\mathit{err}:\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}Q_{\mathit{err}}\lor\mathsf{False}\lor\mathsf{False}\end{array}\right)}

and via equivalence the desired

\small{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left(\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(x,\mathit{xs})\end{array}\right)}\ {\mathtt{LClient}(\mathsf{x})}\ {\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left(\mathit{ok}:\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}Q_{\mathit{ok}}\end{array}\right)}\ {\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left(\mathit{err}:\begin{array}[]{@{}c@{}}Q_{\mathit{err}}\end{array}\right)}

F.8
Binary Search Tree: BSTFindMin

Now, we illustrate reasoning about binary search tree (BST) algorithms. We start with the following search algorithm, where we omit the case when the tree is empty to make the proof shorter and more focused:

\begin{array}[]{l}\mathtt{BSTFindMin}(\mathsf{x})~\{\\
\quad\!\!\mathsf{l}\mathtt{\ :=\ }[\mathsf{x}+1];\\
\quad\!\!\mathtt{if}\ (\mathsf{l}=\mathtt{null})\ \{\\
\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\mathsf{min}\mathtt{\ :=\ }[\mathsf{x}]\\
\quad\!\!\}\ \mathtt{else}\ \{\\
\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\mathsf{min}\mathtt{\ :=\ }\mathtt{BSTFindMin}(\mathsf{l})\\
\quad\!\!\};\\
\quad\!\!\mathtt{return}\ \mathsf{min}\\
\}\\
\end{array}

The specification we would like to prove is:

\begin{array}[]{c}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}x\neq\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(x,K))}\\
{\mathtt{BSTFindMin}(\mathsf{x})}\\
{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(x\neq\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(x,K)\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{ret}=\min(K))}\end{array}

The proof sketch is as follows, using the measure that
\alpha
is set-cardinality
\|K\|
:

\small\begin{array}[]{l}\Gamma(\alpha)\vdash\\
{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}x\neq\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(x,K)\mathrel{\star}\alpha=\|K\|\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{l},\mathsf{min}=\mathtt{null}\end{array}~)}\\
{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\exists k,l,r,K_{l},K_{r}\ldotp\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}x\mapsto k,l,r\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(r,K_{r})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(l,K_{l})\mathrel{\star}K=K_{l}\uplus\{k\}\uplus K_{r}\mathrel{\star}K_{l}<k\mathrel{\star}k<K_{r}\mathrel{\star}\\
\alpha=\|K\|\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{l},\mathsf{min}=\mathtt{null}\end{array}~\right)}\\
\mathsf{l}\mathtt{\ :=\ }[\mathsf{x}+1];\\
{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\exists k,l,r,K_{l},K_{r}\ldotp\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}x\mapsto k,l,r\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(r,K_{r})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(l,K_{l})\mathrel{\star}K=K_{l}\uplus\{k\}\uplus K_{r}\mathrel{\star}K_{l}<k\mathrel{\star}k<K_{r}\mathrel{\star}\\
\alpha=\|K\|\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{l}=l\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{min}=\mathtt{null}\end{array}~\right)}\\
\mathtt{if}\ (\mathsf{l}=\mathtt{null})\ \{\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\exists k,l,r,K_{l},K_{r}\ldotp\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}x\mapsto k,l,r\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(r,K_{r})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(l,K_{l})\mathrel{\star}K=K_{l}\uplus\{k\}\uplus K_{r}\mathrel{\star}K_{l}<k\mathrel{\star}k<K_{r}\mathrel{\star}\\
\alpha=\|K\|\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{l}=l\mathrel{\star}l=\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{min}=\mathtt{null}\end{array}~\right)}\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\mathsf{min}\mathtt{\ :=\ }[\mathsf{x}]\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\exists k,l,r,K_{l},K_{r}\ldotp\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}x\mapsto k,l,r\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(r,K_{r})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(l,K_{l})\mathrel{\star}K=K_{l}\uplus\{k\}\uplus K_{r}\mathrel{\star}K_{l}<k\mathrel{\star}k<K_{r}\mathrel{\star}\\
\alpha=\|K\|\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{l}=l\mathrel{\star}l=\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{min}=k\end{array}~\right)}\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\text{{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}// $\mathsf{BST}(l,K_{l})\mathrel{\star}l=\mathtt{null}\Rightarrow K_{l}=\emptyset\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{True}$ which with $K=K_{l}\uplus\{k\}\uplus K_{r}$ and $k<K_{r}$ implies $k=\min{K}$}}\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\exists k,l,r,K_{l},K_{r}\ldotp\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}x\mapsto k,l,r\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(r,K_{r})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(l,K_{l})\mathrel{\star}K=K_{l}\uplus\{k\}\uplus K_{r}\mathrel{\star}K_{l}<k\mathrel{\star}k<K_{r}\mathrel{\star}\\
\alpha=\|K\|\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{l}=l\mathrel{\star}l=\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{min}=\min(K)\end{array}~\right)}\\
\}\ \mathtt{else}\ \{\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\exists k,l,r,K_{l},K_{r}\ldotp\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}x\mapsto k,l,r\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(r,K_{r})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(l,K_{l})\mathrel{\star}K=K_{l}\uplus\{k\}\uplus K_{r}\mathrel{\star}K_{l}<k\mathrel{\star}k<K_{r}\mathrel{\star}\\
\alpha=\|K\|\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{l}=l\mathrel{\star}l\neq\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{min}=\mathtt{null}\end{array}~\right)}\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\text{{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}// $K_{l}$ is a strict subset of $K$ and therefore of strictly smaller cardinality}}\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\begin{array}[]{@{}m{10pt}|l@{}}\text{\rotatebox{90.0}{\ {fr + ex}\ }&\begin{array}[]{@{}l@{}}\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{min}=\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{l}=l\mathrel{\star}l\neq\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(l,K_{l})\mathrel{\star}\alpha>\|K_{l}\|\end{array}~)}\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\mathsf{min}\mathtt{\ :=\ }\mathtt{BSTFindMin}(\mathsf{l})\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{min}=\min(K_{l})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{l}=l\mathrel{\star}l\neq\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(l,K_{l})\mathrel{\star}\alpha>\|K_{l}\|\end{array}~)}\end{array}\end{array}\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\exists k,l,r,K_{l},K_{r}\ldotp\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}x\mapsto k,l,r\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(r,K_{r})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(l,K_{l})\mathrel{\star}K=K_{l}\uplus\{k\}\uplus K_{r}\mathrel{\star}K_{l}<k\mathrel{\star}k<K_{r}\mathrel{\star}\\
\alpha=\|K\|\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{l}=l\mathrel{\star}l\neq\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{min}=\min(K_{l})\end{array}~\right)}\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\text{{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}// $K=K_{l}\uplus\{k\}\uplus K_{r}$, $K_{l}<k$, $k<K_{r}$ and $\min(K_{l})=\min(K)$}}\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\exists k,l,r,K_{l},K_{r}\ldotp\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}x\mapsto k,l,r\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(r,K_{r})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(l,K_{l})\mathrel{\star}K=K_{l}\uplus\{k\}\uplus K_{r}\mathrel{\star}K_{l}<k\mathrel{\star}k<K_{r}\mathrel{\star}\\
\alpha=\|K\|\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{l}=l\mathrel{\star}l\neq\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{min}=\min(K)\end{array}~\right)}\\
\};\\
{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}(\exists k,l,r,K_{l},K_{r}\ldotp\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}x\mapsto k,l,r\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(r,K_{r})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(l,K_{l})\mathrel{\star}K=K_{l}\uplus\{k\}\uplus K_{r}\mathrel{\star}K_{l}<k\mathrel{\star}k<K_{r}\mathrel{\star}\\
\alpha=\|K\|\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{l}=l\mathrel{\star}l=\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{min}=\min(K))~\vee\\
(\exists k,l,r,K_{l},K_{r}\ldotp\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}x\mapsto k,l,r\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(r,K_{r})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(l,K_{l})\mathrel{\star}K=K_{l}\uplus\{k\}\uplus K_{r}\mathrel{\star}K_{l}<k\mathrel{\star}k<K_{r}\mathrel{\star}\\
\alpha=\|K\|\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{l}=l\mathrel{\star}l\neq\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{min}=\min(K))\end{array}~\right)}\\
{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\exists k,l,r,K_{l},K_{r}\ldotp\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}x\mapsto k,l,r\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(r,K_{r})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(l,K_{l})\mathrel{\star}K=K_{l}\uplus\{k\}\uplus K_{r}\mathrel{\star}K_{l}<k\mathrel{\star}k<K_{r}\mathrel{\star}\\
\alpha=\|K\|\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{l}=l\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{min}=\min(K)\mathrel{\star}(l=\mathtt{null}\vee l\neq\mathtt{null})\end{array}~\right)}\\
{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left(~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\exists k,l,r,K_{l},K_{r}\ldotp\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}x\mapsto k,l,r\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(r,K_{r})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(l,K_{l})\mathrel{\star}K=K_{l}\uplus\{k\}\uplus K_{r}\mathrel{\star}K_{l}<k\mathrel{\star}k<K_{r}\mathrel{\star}\\
\alpha=\|K\|\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{l}=l\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{min}=\min(K)\end{array}~\right)}\\
\mathtt{return}\ \mathsf{min}\\
\end{array}}

Lastly, we need to prove the connection between the interior and exterior post-conditions:

\begin{array}[]{l l}&\exists p_{l},p_{min},p_{x},k,l,r,K_{l},K_{r}\ldotp p_{x}=x\mathrel{\star}x\mapsto k,l,r\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(r,K_{r})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(l,K_{l})\mathrel{\star}\\
&K=K_{l}\uplus\{k\}\uplus K_{r}\mathrel{\star}K_{l}<k\mathrel{\star}k<K_{r}\mathrel{\star}\alpha=\|K\|\mathrel{\star}p_{l}=l\mathrel{\star}p_{min}=\min(K)\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{ret}=p_{min}\\
\Leftrightarrow&\exists k,l,r,K_{l},K_{r}\ldotp x\mapsto k,l,r\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(r,K_{r})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(l,K_{l})\mathrel{\star}\\
&K=K_{l}\uplus\{k\}\uplus K_{r}\mathrel{\star}K_{l}<k\mathrel{\star}k<K_{r}\mathrel{\star}\alpha=\|K\|\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{ret}=\min(K)\\
\Leftrightarrow&x\neq\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(x,K)\mathrel{\star}\alpha=\|K\|\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{ret}=\min(K)\\
\end{array}

F.9
Binary Search Tree: BSTInsert

Next, we consider a function that inserts a value into a BST:

\begin{array}[]{l}\mathtt{BSTInsert}(\mathsf{v},\mathsf{x})~\{\\
\quad\!\!\mathtt{if}\ (\mathsf{x}=\mathtt{null})\ \{\\
\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\mathsf{x}\mathtt{\ :=\ }\mathtt{new}(3);[\mathsf{x}]\mathtt{\ :=\ }\mathsf{v}\\
\quad\!\!\}\ \mathtt{else}\ \{\\
\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\mathsf{k}\mathtt{\ :=\ }[\mathsf{x}];\\
\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\mathtt{if}\ (\mathsf{k}=\mathsf{v})\ \{\\
\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\mathtt{skip}\\
\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\}\ \mathtt{else}\ \{\\
\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\mathtt{if}\ (\mathsf{v}<\mathsf{k})\ \{\\
\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\mathsf{y}\mathtt{\ :=\ }[\mathsf{x}+1];\mathsf{y}\mathtt{\ :=\ }\mathtt{BSTInsert}(\mathsf{v},\mathsf{y});[\mathsf{x}+1]\mathtt{\ :=\ }\mathsf{y}\\
\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\}\ \mathtt{else}\ \{\\
\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\mathsf{y}\mathtt{\ :=\ }[\mathsf{x}+2];\mathsf{y}\mathtt{\ :=\ }\mathtt{BSTInsert}(\mathsf{v},\mathsf{y});[\mathsf{x}+2]\mathtt{\ :=\ }\mathsf{y}\\
\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\}\\
\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\}\\
\quad\!\!\};\\
\quad\!\!\mathtt{return}\ \mathsf{x}\\
\}\end{array}

For the specification, we require the
\mathsf{BSTRoot}(x,\tau)
stating that
x
is the root of the tree
\tau
, for the same reason we need the
\mathsf{listHead}(x,\alpha)
predicate for some list algorithms:

\mathsf{BSTRoot}(x,\tau)\triangleq(\tau=\tau_{\emptyset}\mathrel{\star}x=\mathtt{null})\lor(\exists k,\tau_{l},\tau_{r}\ldotp\tau=((x,k),\tau_{l},\tau_{r}))

For completeness, we also give a functional description of our mathematical
\mathsf{BSTInsert}(\tau,(x^{\prime},v))
function:

\begin{array}[]{l}\mathsf{BSTInsert}(\tau_{\emptyset},(x^{\prime},v))\triangleq((x^{\prime},v),\tau_{\emptyset},\tau_{\emptyset})\\[5.69054pt]
\mathsf{BSTInsert}(((x,k),\tau_{l},\tau_{r}),(x^{\prime},v))\triangleq\\
\qquad\mathsf{if}\ v<k\ \mathsf{then}\\
\qquad\quad((x,k),\mathsf{BSTInsert}(\tau_{l},(x^{\prime},v)),\tau_{r})\\
\qquad\mathsf{else\ if}\ k<v\ \mathsf{then}\\
\qquad\quad((x,k),\tau_{l},\mathsf{BSTInsert}(\tau_{r},(x^{\prime},v)))\\
\qquad\mathsf{else}\\
\qquad\quad((x,k),\tau_{l},\tau_{r})\end{array}

The specification we would like to prove is:

\small\begin{array}[]{c}{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{v}=v\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(x,\tau))}\\
{\mathtt{BSTInsert}(\mathsf{x},\mathsf{v})}\\
{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}(\exists x^{\prime}.~\mathsf{BST}(\mathsf{ret},\mathsf{BSTInsert}(\tau,(x^{\prime},v)))\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BSTRoot}(x,\tau))}\end{array}

The proof sketch is as follows, using the measure that
\alpha
equals
\mathsf{height}(\tau)
:

\small\begin{array}[]{l}\mathtt{BSTInsert}(\mathsf{v},\mathsf{x})~\{\\
\hskip-17.92537pt\Gamma(\alpha)\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left\{~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{v}=v\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(x,\tau)\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{k},\mathsf{y}=\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\alpha=\mathsf{height}(\tau)\end{array}~\right\}}\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\mathtt{if}\ (\mathsf{x}=\mathtt{null})\ \{\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left\{~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{v}=v\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(x,\tau)\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{k},\mathsf{y}=\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\hbox{\pagecolor{slgray}$\mathsf{x}=\mathtt{null}$}\mathrel{\star}\alpha=\mathsf{height}(\tau)\end{array}~\right\}}\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left\{~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{v}=v\mathrel{\star}\hbox{\pagecolor{slgray}$\mathsf{BSTRoot}(x,\tau)\mathrel{\star}\tau=\tau_{\emptyset}$}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{k},\mathsf{y}=\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\alpha=\mathsf{height}(\tau)\end{array}~\right\}}\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\mathsf{x}\mathtt{\ :=\ }\mathtt{new}(3);[\mathsf{x}]\mathtt{\ :=\ }\mathsf{v}\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left\{~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\hbox{\pagecolor{slgray}$\mathsf{x}\mapsto v,\mathtt{null},\mathtt{null}$}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{v}=v\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BSTRoot}(x,\tau)\mathrel{\star}\tau=\tau_{\emptyset}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{k},\mathsf{y}=\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\alpha=\mathsf{height}(\tau)\end{array}~\right\}}\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\text{{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}// $\mathsf{x}\mapsto v,\mathtt{null},\mathtt{null}$ is equivalent to $\mathsf{BST}(\mathsf{x},((\mathsf{x},v),\tau_{\emptyset},\tau_{\emptyset}))$}}\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\text{{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}// When $\tau=\tau_{\emptyset}$, $\mathsf{BSTInsert}(\tau,(\mathsf{x},v))=((\mathsf{x},v),\tau_{\emptyset},\tau_{\emptyset})$}}\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left\{~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}Q_{1}:~\hbox{\pagecolor{slgray}$\mathsf{BST}(\mathsf{x},\mathsf{BSTInsert}(\tau,(\mathsf{x},v)))$}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BSTRoot}(x,\tau)\mathrel{\star}\alpha=\mathsf{height}(\tau)\mathrel{\star}\tau=\tau_{\emptyset}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{v}=v\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{k},\mathsf{y}=\mathtt{null}\end{array}~\right\}}\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\}\ \mathtt{else}\ \{\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left\{~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{v}=v\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(x,\tau)\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{k},\mathsf{y}=\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\hbox{\pagecolor{slgray}$\mathsf{x}\mathbin{\neq}\mathtt{null}$}\mathrel{\star}\alpha=\mathsf{height}(\tau)\end{array}~\right\}}\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\text{{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}// Unfold BST, add on $\mathsf{BSTRoot}(x,\tau)$ by equivalence }}\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left\{~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\hbox{\pagecolor{slgray}$\exists k,l,r,\tau_{l},\tau_{r}\ldotp$}~\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{v}=v\mathrel{\star}\hbox{\pagecolor{slgray}$x\mapsto k,l,r\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(r,\tau_{r})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(l,\tau_{l})\mathrel{\star}\tau=((x,k),\tau_{l},\tau_{r})\mathrel{\star}$}\\
\hbox{\pagecolor{slgray}$\tau_{l}<k\mathrel{\star}k<\tau_{r}\star\mathsf{BSTRoot}(x,\tau)$}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{k},\mathsf{y}=\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\alpha=\mathsf{height}(\tau)\end{array}~\right\}}\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\mathsf{k}\mathtt{\ :=\ }[\mathsf{x}];\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\text{{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}// $\mathsf{k}$ in place of $k$, dropping the existential}}\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left\{~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\exists l,r,\tau_{l},\tau_{r}\ldotp\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{v}=v\mathrel{\star}x\mapsto\mathsf{k},l,r\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(r,\tau_{r})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(l,\tau_{l})\mathrel{\star}\tau=((x,\mathsf{k}),\tau_{l},\tau_{r})\mathrel{\star}\\
\tau_{l}<\mathsf{k}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{k}<\tau_{r}\star\mathsf{BSTRoot}(x,\tau)\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{y}=\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\alpha=\mathsf{height}(\tau)\end{array}~\right\}}\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\mathtt{if}\ (\mathsf{k}=\mathsf{v})\ \{\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left\{~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\exists l,r,\tau_{l},\tau_{r}\ldotp\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{v}=v\mathrel{\star}x\mapsto\mathsf{k},l,r\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(r,\tau_{r})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(l,\tau_{l})\mathrel{\star}\tau=((x,\mathsf{k}),\tau_{l},\tau_{r})\mathrel{\star}\\
\tau_{l}<\mathsf{k}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{k}<\tau_{r}\star\mathsf{BSTRoot}(x,\tau)\mathrel{\star}\hbox{\pagecolor{slgray}$\mathsf{k}=\mathsf{v}$}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{y}=\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\alpha=\mathsf{height}(\tau)\end{array}~\right\}}\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\mathtt{skip}\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left\{~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}Q_{2}:\exists l,r,\tau_{l},\tau_{r}\ldotp\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{v}=v\mathrel{\star}x\mapsto\mathsf{k},l,r\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(r,\tau_{r})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(l,\tau_{l})\mathrel{\star}\tau=((x,\mathsf{k}),\tau_{l},\tau_{r})\mathrel{\star}\\
\tau_{l}<\mathsf{k}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{k}<\tau_{r}\star\mathsf{BSTRoot}(x,\tau)\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{k}=\mathsf{v}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{y}=\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\alpha=\mathsf{height}(\tau)\end{array}~\right\}}\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\}\ \mathtt{else}\ \{\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left\{~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\exists l,r,\tau_{l},\tau_{r}\ldotp\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{v}=v\mathrel{\star}x\mapsto\mathsf{k},l,r\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(r,\tau_{r})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(l,\tau_{l})\mathrel{\star}\tau=((x,\mathsf{k}),\tau_{l},\tau_{r})\mathrel{\star}\\
\tau_{l}<\mathsf{k}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{k}<\tau_{r}\star\mathsf{BSTRoot}(x,\tau)\mathrel{\star}\hbox{\pagecolor{slgray}$\mathsf{k}\mathbin{\neq}\mathsf{v}$}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{y}=\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\alpha=\mathsf{height}(\tau)\end{array}~\right\}}\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\mathtt{if}\ (\mathsf{v}<\mathsf{k})\ \{\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left\{~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\exists l,r,\tau_{l},\tau_{r}\ldotp\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{v}=v\mathrel{\star}x\mapsto\mathsf{k},l,r\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(r,\tau_{r})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(l,\tau_{l})\mathrel{\star}\tau=((x,\mathsf{k}),\tau_{l},\tau_{r})\mathrel{\star}\\
\tau_{l}<\mathsf{k}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{k}<\tau_{r}\star\mathsf{BSTRoot}(x,\tau)\mathrel{\star}\hbox{\pagecolor{slgray}$\mathsf{v}\mathbin{<}\mathsf{k}$}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{y}=\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\alpha=\mathsf{height}(\tau)\end{array}~\right\}}\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\mathsf{y}\mathtt{\ :=\ }[\mathsf{x}+1];\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left\{~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\exists l,r,\tau_{l},\tau_{r}\ldotp\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{v}=v\mathrel{\star}x\mapsto\mathsf{k},l,r\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(r,\tau_{r})\mathrel{\star}\hbox{\pagecolor{slgray}$\mathsf{y}=l\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(l,\tau_{l})\mathrel{\star}\alpha-1=\mathsf{height}(\tau_{l})$}\mathrel{\star}\\
\tau=((x,\mathsf{k}),\tau_{l},\tau_{r})\mathrel{\star}\tau_{l}<\mathsf{k}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{k}<\tau_{r}\star\mathsf{BSTRoot}(x,\tau)\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{v}\mathbin{<}\mathsf{k}\mathrel{\star}\alpha=\mathsf{height}(\tau)\end{array}~\right\}}\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\mathsf{y}\mathtt{\ :=\ }\mathtt{BSTInsert}(\mathsf{v},\mathsf{y});\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left\{~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\exists l,r,\tau_{l},\tau_{r}\ldotp\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{v}=v\mathrel{\star}x\mapsto\mathsf{k},l,r\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(r,\tau_{r})\mathrel{\star}\\
\hbox{\pagecolor{slgray}$(\exists x^{\prime}.~\mathsf{BST}(\mathsf{y},\mathsf{BSTInsert}(\tau_{l},(x^{\prime},v)))\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BSTRoot}(l,\tau_{l})\mathrel{\star}\alpha-1=\mathsf{height}(\tau_{l}))$}\mathrel{\star}\\
\tau=((x,\mathsf{k}),\tau_{l},\tau_{r})\mathrel{\star}\tau_{l}<\mathsf{k}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{k}<\tau_{r}\star\mathsf{BSTRoot}(x,\tau)\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{v}\mathbin{<}\mathsf{k}\mathrel{\star}\alpha=\mathsf{height}(\tau)\end{array}~\right\}}\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\![\mathsf{x}+1]\mathtt{\ :=\ }\mathsf{y}\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left\{~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\exists l,r,\tau_{l},\tau_{r}\ldotp\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{v}=v\mathrel{\star}x\mapsto\mathsf{k},\hbox{\pagecolor{slgray}$\mathsf{y}$},r\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(r,\tau_{r})\mathrel{\star}\\
{(\exists x^{\prime}.~\mathsf{BST}(\mathsf{y},\mathsf{BSTInsert}(\tau_{l},(x^{\prime},v)))\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BSTRoot}(l,\tau_{l})\mathrel{\star}\alpha-1=\mathsf{height}(\tau_{l}))}\mathrel{\star}\\
\tau=((x,\mathsf{k}),\tau_{l},\tau_{r})\mathrel{\star}\tau_{l}<\mathsf{k}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{k}<\tau_{r}\star\mathsf{BSTRoot}(x,\tau)\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{v}\mathbin{<}\mathsf{k}\mathrel{\star}\alpha=\mathsf{height}(\tau)\end{array}~\right\}}\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\text{{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}// Forget $\alpha-1=\mathsf{height}(\tau_{l})$, as it can be entailed}}\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\text{{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}// Forget $\exists l.~\mathsf{BSTRoot}(l,\tau_{l})$, as it is a tautology}}\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\text{{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}// Re-instate the existential $l$ it as the value of $\mathsf{y}$}}\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left\{~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}Q_{3}:\exists x^{\prime},l,r,\tau_{l},\tau_{r}\ldotp\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{y}=l\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{v}=v\mathrel{\star}x\mapsto\mathsf{k},l,r\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(l,\mathsf{BSTInsert}(\tau_{l},(x^{\prime},v)))\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(r,\tau_{r})\mathrel{\star}\\
\tau=((x,\mathsf{k}),\tau_{l},\tau_{r})\mathrel{\star}\tau_{l}<\mathsf{k}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{k}<\tau_{r}\star\mathsf{BSTRoot}(x,\tau)\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{v}\mathbin{<}\mathsf{k}\mathrel{\star}\alpha=\mathsf{height}(\tau)\end{array}~\right\}}\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\}\ \mathtt{else}\ \{\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left\{~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\exists l,r,\tau_{l},\tau_{r}\ldotp\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{v}=v\mathrel{\star}x\mapsto\mathsf{k},l,r\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(r,\tau_{r})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(l,\tau_{l})\mathrel{\star}\tau=((x,\mathsf{k}),\tau_{l},\tau_{r})\mathrel{\star}\\
\tau_{l}<\mathsf{k}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{k}<\tau_{r}\star\mathsf{BSTRoot}(x,\tau)\mathrel{\star}\hbox{\pagecolor{slgray}$\mathsf{v}\mathbin{>}\mathsf{k}$}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{y}=\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\alpha=\mathsf{height}(\tau)\end{array}~\right\}}\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\mathsf{y}\mathtt{\ :=\ }[\mathsf{x}+2];\mathsf{y}\mathtt{\ :=\ }\mathtt{BSTInsert}(\mathsf{v},\mathsf{y});[\mathsf{x}+2]\mathtt{\ :=\ }\mathsf{y}\\
\end{array}

\small\begin{array}[]{l}\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\text{{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}// Analogously to previous branch}}\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left\{~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}Q_{4}:\exists x^{\prime},l,r,\tau_{l},\tau_{r}\ldotp\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{y}=r\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{v}=v\mathrel{\star}x\mapsto\mathsf{k},l,r\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(l,\tau_{l})\mathrel{\star}\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\mathsf{BST}(r,\mathsf{BSTInsert}(\tau_{r},(x^{\prime},v)))\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BSTRoot}(x_{r},\tau_{r})\mathrel{\star}\tau=((x,\mathsf{k}),\tau_{l},\tau_{r})\mathrel{\star}\tau_{l}<\mathsf{k}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{k}<\tau_{r}\star\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\mathsf{BSTRoot}(x,\tau)\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{v}\mathbin{>}\mathsf{k}\mathrel{\star}\alpha=\mathsf{height}(\tau)\end{array}~\right\}}\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\}\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\}\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\mathtt{return}\ \mathsf{x}\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left\{~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\exists x^{\prime},v^{\prime},k^{\prime},y^{\prime}.~((Q_{1}\lor Q_{2}\lor Q_{3}\lor Q_{4})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{ret}=\mathsf{x})[x^{\prime},v^{\prime},k^{\prime},y^{\prime}/\mathsf{x},\mathsf{v},\mathsf{k},\mathsf{y}]\end{array}~\right\}}\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left\{~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}(\exists x^{\prime},v^{\prime},k^{\prime},y^{\prime}.~(Q_{1}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{ret}=\mathsf{x})[x^{\prime},v^{\prime},k^{\prime},y^{\prime}/\mathsf{x},\mathsf{v},\mathsf{k},\mathsf{y}])~\lor\\
(\exists x^{\prime},v^{\prime},k^{\prime},y^{\prime}.~(Q_{2}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{ret}=\mathsf{x})[x^{\prime},v^{\prime},k^{\prime},y^{\prime}/\mathsf{x},\mathsf{v},\mathsf{k},\mathsf{y}])~\lor\\
(\exists x^{\prime},v^{\prime},k^{\prime},y^{\prime}.~(Q_{3}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{ret}=\mathsf{x})[x^{\prime},v^{\prime},k^{\prime},y^{\prime}/\mathsf{x},\mathsf{v},\mathsf{k},\mathsf{y}])~\lor\\
(\exists x^{\prime},v^{\prime},k^{\prime},y^{\prime}.~(Q_{4}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{ret}=\mathsf{x})[x^{\prime},v^{\prime},k^{\prime},y^{\prime}/\mathsf{x},\mathsf{v},\mathsf{k},\mathsf{y}])\end{array}~\right\}}\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left\{~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}(Q(\alpha)\mathrel{\star}\tau=\tau_{\emptyset})~\lor\\
(Q(\alpha)\mathrel{\star}(\exists\tau_{l},\tau_{r}.~\tau=((x,v),\tau_{l},\tau_{r})))~\lor\\
(Q(\alpha)\mathrel{\star}(\exists k,\tau_{l},\tau_{r}.~\tau=((x,k),\tau_{l},\tau_{r})\mathrel{\star}v\mathbin{<}k))~\lor\\
(Q(\alpha)\mathrel{\star}(\exists k,\tau_{l},\tau_{r}.~\tau=((x,k),\tau_{l},\tau_{r})\mathrel{\star}v\mathbin{>}k))\end{array}~\right\}}\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!\text{{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}// The four additional assertions cover the space of possibilities }}\\
\hskip 9.24994pt\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left\{~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}Q(\alpha)\end{array}~\right\}}\\
\}\\
{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left\{~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}Q(\alpha)\end{array}~\right\}}\\
\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{r@{~}l}&\exists x^{\prime},v^{\prime},k^{\prime},y^{\prime}.~(Q_{1}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{ret}=\mathsf{x})[x^{\prime},v^{\prime},k^{\prime},y^{\prime}/\mathsf{x},\mathsf{v},\mathsf{k},\mathsf{y}]\\
\Leftrightarrow&\exists...~\mathsf{BST}(x^{\prime},\mathsf{BSTInsert}(\tau,(x^{\prime},v)))\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BSTRoot}(x,\tau)\mathrel{\star}\alpha=\mathsf{height}(\tau)\mathrel{\star}\tau=\tau_{\emptyset}\mathrel{\star}v^{\prime}=v\mathrel{\star}\\
&\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\quad\!\!k^{\prime},y^{\prime}=\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{ret}=x^{\prime}\\
&\text{{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}// Drop $v^{\prime}$, $k^{\prime}$, $y^{\prime}$ }}\\
\Leftrightarrow&\exists x^{\prime}.~\mathsf{BST}(x^{\prime},\mathsf{BSTInsert}(\tau,(x^{\prime},v)))\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BSTRoot}(x,\tau)\mathrel{\star}\alpha=\mathsf{height}(\tau)\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{ret}=x^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\tau=\tau_{\emptyset}\\
&\text{{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}// Substitute $\mathsf{ret}$ for $x^{\prime}$ in first parameter of BST}}\\
&\text{{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}// Lose $\mathsf{ret}=x^{\prime}$, it is entailed given definition of BSTInsert when $\tau=\tau_{\emptyset}$ }}\\
\Leftrightarrow&\exists x^{\prime}.~\mathsf{BST}(\mathsf{ret},\mathsf{BSTInsert}(\tau,(x^{\prime},v)))\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BSTRoot}(x,\tau)\mathrel{\star}\alpha=\mathsf{height}(\tau)\mathrel{\star}\tau=\tau_{\emptyset}\\
\Leftrightarrow&Q(\alpha)\mathrel{\star}\tau=\tau_{\emptyset}\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{r@{~}l}&\exists x^{\prime},v^{\prime},k^{\prime},y^{\prime}.~(Q_{2}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{ret}=\mathsf{x})[x^{\prime},v^{\prime},k^{\prime},y^{\prime}/\mathsf{x},\mathsf{v},\mathsf{k},\mathsf{y}]\\
\Leftrightarrow&\exists x^{\prime},v^{\prime},k^{\prime},y^{\prime},l,r,\tau_{l},\tau_{r}.\\
&x^{\prime}=x\mathrel{\star}v^{\prime}=v\mathrel{\star}x\mapsto k^{\prime},l,r\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(r,\tau_{r})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(l,\tau_{l})\mathrel{\star}\tau=((x,k^{\prime}),\tau_{l},\tau_{r})\mathrel{\star}\\
&\tau_{l}<k^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}k^{\prime}<\tau_{r}\star\mathsf{BSTRoot}(x,\tau)\mathrel{\star}k^{\prime}=v^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}y^{\prime}=\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}\alpha=\mathsf{height}(\tau)\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{ret}=x^{\prime}\\
\Leftrightarrow&\exists x^{\prime},k^{\prime},l,r,\tau_{l},\tau_{r}.\\
&\text{{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}// Drop $v^{\prime}$ (keeping $k^{\prime}=v$), $y^{\prime}$ }}\\
&\text{{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}// Add $Q_{\mathit{aux}}:(\exists\tau_{l},\tau_{r}.~\tau=((x,v),\tau_{l},\tau_{r}))$ (it can be entailed)}}\\
&\text{{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}// Substitute $x$ for $x^{\prime}$ in the unfolding of BST}}\\
&\text{{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}// Forget $x^{\prime}=x$ and $k^{\prime}=v$, as they can be entailed given rest and $Q_{\mathit{aux}}$}}\\
&x^{\prime}\mapsto k^{\prime},l,r\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(r,\tau_{r})\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(l,\tau_{l})\mathrel{\star}\tau=((x^{\prime},k^{\prime}),\tau_{l},\tau_{r})\mathrel{\star}\\
&\tau_{l}<k^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}k^{\prime}<\tau_{r}\star\mathsf{BSTRoot}(x,\tau)\mathrel{\star}\alpha=\mathsf{height}(\tau)\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{ret}=x^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}(\exists\tau_{l},\tau_{r}.~\tau=((x,v),\tau_{l},\tau_{r}))\\
&\text{{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}// Fold $\mathsf{BST}(x^{\prime},\tau)$; by definition, $\mathsf{BSTInsert}(\tau,(x^{\prime},v))=\tau$ when $Q_{\mathit{aux}}$ holds}}\\
\Leftrightarrow&\exists x^{\prime}.~\mathsf{BST}(x^{\prime},\mathsf{BSTInsert}(\tau,(x^{\prime},v)))\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BSTRoot}(x,\tau)\mathrel{\star}\alpha=\mathsf{height}(\tau)\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{ret}=x^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\\
&\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\quad\!\!\quad\!\!(\exists\tau_{l},\tau_{r}.~\tau=((x,v),\tau_{l},\tau_{r}))\\
\Leftrightarrow&Q(\alpha)\mathrel{\star}(\exists\tau_{l},\tau_{r}.~\tau=((x,v),\tau_{l},\tau_{r}))\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{r@{~}l}&\exists x^{\prime},v^{\prime},k^{\prime},y^{\prime}.~(Q_{3}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{ret}=\mathsf{x})[x^{\prime},v^{\prime},k^{\prime},y^{\prime}/\mathsf{x},\mathsf{v},\mathsf{k},\mathsf{y}]\\
