\Leftrightarrow&\exists x^{\prime},v^{\prime},k^{\prime},y^{\prime},l,r,\tau_{l},\tau_{r},x^{\prime\prime}.\\
&x^{\prime}=x\mathrel{\star}y^{\prime}=l\mathrel{\star}v^{\prime}=v\mathrel{\star}x\mapsto k^{\prime},l,r\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(l,\mathsf{BSTInsert}(\tau_{l},(x^{\prime\prime},v)))\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(r,\tau_{r})\mathrel{\star}\\
&\tau=((x,k^{\prime}),\tau_{l},\tau_{r})\mathrel{\star}\tau_{l}<k^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}k^{\prime}<\tau_{r}\star\mathsf{BSTRoot}(x,\tau)\mathrel{\star}v^{\prime}\mathbin{<}k^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\alpha=\mathsf{height}(\tau)\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{ret}=x^{\prime}\\
\Leftrightarrow&\exists x^{\prime},x^{\prime\prime},k^{\prime},l,r,\tau_{l},\tau_{r}.\\
&\text{{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}// Drop $v^{\prime}$, $y^{\prime}$ }}\\
&\text{{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}// Add $Q_{\mathit{aux}}:(\exists k,\tau_{l},\tau_{r}.~\tau=((x,k),\tau_{l},\tau_{r})\mathrel{\star}v\mathbin{<}k)$ (it can be entailed)}}\\
&\text{{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}// Substitute $x$ for $x^{\prime}$ in the unfolding of BST}}\\
&\text{{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}// Forget $x^{\prime}=x$ as it can be entailed given rest and $Q_{\mathit{aux}}$}}\\
&x^{\prime}\mapsto k^{\prime},l,r\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(l,\mathsf{BSTInsert}(\tau_{l},(x^{\prime\prime},v)))\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(r,\tau_{r})\mathrel{\star}\tau=((x^{\prime},k^{\prime}),\tau_{l},\tau_{r})\mathrel{\star}\\
&\tau_{l}<k^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}k^{\prime}<\tau_{r}\star\mathsf{BSTRoot}(x,\tau)\mathrel{\star}v\mathbin{<}k^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\alpha=\mathsf{height}(\tau)\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{ret}=x^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\\
&(\exists k,\tau_{l},\tau_{r}.~\tau=((x,k),\tau_{l},\tau_{r})\mathrel{\star}v\mathbin{<}k)\\
\Leftrightarrow&\exists x^{\prime},x^{\prime\prime},k^{\prime},l,r,\tau_{l},\tau_{r}.\\
&\text{{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}// Set up folding using the definition of BSTInsert and the present inequalities}}\\
&\text{{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}// Drop $v<k^{\prime}$ as it can be entailed given rest and $Q_{\mathit{aux}}$}}\\
&x^{\prime}\mapsto k^{\prime},l,r\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(l,\mathsf{BSTInsert}(\tau_{l},(x^{\prime\prime},v)))\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(r,\tau_{r})\mathrel{\star}\\
&\mathsf{BSTInsert}(\tau,(x^{\prime\prime},v))=((x,k),\mathsf{BSTInsert}(\tau_{l},(x^{\prime\prime},v)),\tau_{r})\mathrel{\star}\\
&\mathsf{BSTInsert}(\tau_{l},(x^{\prime\prime},v))<k^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}k^{\prime}<\tau_{r}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BSTRoot}(x,\tau)\mathrel{\star}\alpha=\mathsf{height}(\tau)\mathrel{\star}\\
&\mathsf{ret}=x^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}(\exists k,\tau_{l},\tau_{r}.~\tau=((x,k),\tau_{l},\tau_{r})\mathrel{\star}v\mathbin{<}k)\\
&\text{{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}// Fold }}\\
\Leftrightarrow&\exists x^{\prime},x^{\prime\prime}.~\mathsf{BST}(x^{\prime},\mathsf{BSTInsert}(\tau,(x^{\prime\prime},v)))\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BSTRoot}(x,\tau)\mathrel{\star}\alpha=\mathsf{height}(\tau)\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{ret}=x^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\\
&(\exists k,\tau_{l},\tau_{r}.~\tau=((x,k),\tau_{l},\tau_{r})\mathrel{\star}v\mathbin{<}k)\\
&\text{{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}// Substitute $\mathsf{ret}$ for $x^{\prime}$, drop $x^{\prime}$, rename $x^{\prime\prime}$ as $x^{\prime}$}}\\
\Leftrightarrow&\exists x^{\prime}.~\mathsf{BST}(\mathsf{ret},\mathsf{BSTInsert}(\tau,(x^{\prime},v)))\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BSTRoot}(x,\tau)\mathrel{\star}\alpha=\mathsf{height}(\tau)\mathrel{\star}\\
&(\exists k,\tau_{l},\tau_{r}.~\tau=((x,k),\tau_{l},\tau_{r})\mathrel{\star}v\mathbin{<}k)\\
\Leftrightarrow&Q(\alpha)\mathrel{\star}(\exists k,\tau_{l},\tau_{r}.~\tau=((x,k),\tau_{l},\tau_{r})\mathrel{\star}v\mathbin{<}k)\end{array}

The reasoning for
Q_{4}
is analogous to that for
Q_{3}
, obtaining

\begin{array}[]{r@{~}l}&\exists x^{\prime},v^{\prime},k^{\prime},y^{\prime}.~(Q_{3}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{ret}=\mathsf{x})[x^{\prime},v^{\prime},k^{\prime},y^{\prime}/\mathsf{x},\mathsf{v},\mathsf{k},\mathsf{y}]\\
\Leftrightarrow&\exists x^{\prime},v^{\prime},k^{\prime},y^{\prime},l,r,\tau_{l},\tau_{r},x^{\prime\prime}.\\
&x^{\prime}=x\mathrel{\star}y^{\prime}=l\mathrel{\star}v^{\prime}=v\mathrel{\star}x\mapsto k^{\prime},l,r\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(l,\mathsf{BSTInsert}(\tau_{l},(x^{\prime\prime},v)))\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{BST}(r,\tau_{r})\mathrel{\star}\\
&\tau=((x,k^{\prime}),\tau_{l},\tau_{r})\mathrel{\star}\tau_{l}<k^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}k^{\prime}<\tau_{r}\star\mathsf{BSTRoot}(x,\tau)\mathrel{\star}v^{\prime}\mathbin{<}k^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\alpha=\mathsf{height}(\tau)\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{ret}=x^{\prime}\\
\Leftrightarrow&...\\
\Leftrightarrow&Q(\alpha)\mathrel{\star}(\exists k,\tau_{l},\tau_{r}.~\tau=((x,k),\tau_{l},\tau_{r})\mathrel{\star}v\mathbin{>}k)\end{array}

Appendix G
SL Proof of List-length Algorithm

\begin{array}[]{l}\hskip-13.08846pt\text{{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}// SL proofs do not require the measure $\alpha$}}\\
\hskip-13.08846pt\Gamma\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left\{~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(x,n)\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{r}=\mathtt{null}\end{array}~\right\}}\\
\mathtt{if}\ (\mathsf{x}=\mathtt{null})\ \{\\
\quad\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left\{~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(x,n)\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{r}=\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}{\mathsf{x}=\mathtt{null}}\end{array}~\right\}}\\
\quad\!\!\text{{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}// Forward consequence: From $\mathsf{x}=x$, $\mathsf{x}=\mathtt{null}$, and $\mathsf{list}(x,n)$, learn that $n=0$. Forget $\mathsf{x}$.}}\\
\quad\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left\{~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{list}(x,n)\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{r}=\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}n=0\end{array}~\right\}}\\
\quad\!\!\mathsf{r}\mathtt{\ :=\ }0\\
\quad\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left\{~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{list}(x,n)\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{r}=0\mathrel{\star}n=0\end{array}~\right\}}\\
\quad\!\!\text{{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}// Forward consequence: From $\mathsf{r}=0$ and $n=0$, learn that $\mathsf{r}=n$. Forget $\mathsf{r}=0$ and $\mathsf{n}=0$.}}\\
\quad\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left\{~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{list}(x,n)\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{r}=n\end{array}~\right\}}\\
\}\ \mathtt{else}\ \{\\
\quad\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left\{~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}{\mathsf{list}(x,n)}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{r}=\mathtt{null}\mathrel{\star}{\mathsf{x}\mathbin{\neq}\mathtt{null}}\end{array}~\right\}}\\
\quad\!\!\text{{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}// Forward consequence: Unfold $\mathsf{list}(x,n)$:}}\\
\quad\!\!\text{{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}// $\models\mathsf{list}(\mathsf{x},n)\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{x}\mathbin{\neq}\mathtt{null}\Rightarrow\exists v,x^{\prime}.~\mathsf{x}\mapsto v,x^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(x^{\prime},n-1)$.}}\\
\quad\!\!\text{{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}// Note, as with the ISL proof, the equivalence in the unfold step has been replaced by}}\\
\quad\!\!\text{{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}// an implication, however, the step remains unchanged modulo this change.}}\\
\quad\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left\{~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}{\exists v,x^{\prime}}.~\mathsf{x}=x\mathrel{\star}{x\mapsto v,x^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(x^{\prime},n-1)}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{r}=\mathtt{null}\end{array}~\right\}}\\
\quad\!\!\mathsf{x}\mathtt{\ :=\ }[\mathsf{x}+1];\\
\quad\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left\{~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\exists v,x^{\prime}.~{\mathsf{x}=x^{\prime}}\mathrel{\star}x\mapsto v,x^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}{\mathsf{list}(x^{\prime},n-1)}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{r}=\mathtt{null}\end{array}~\right\}}\\
\quad\!\!\mathsf{r}\mathtt{\ :=\ }\mathtt{LLen}(\mathsf{x});\\
\quad\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left\{~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\hbox{\pagecolor{slgray}$\exists v,x^{\prime}$}.~\mathsf{x}=x^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\hbox{\pagecolor{slgray}$x\mapsto v,x^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(x^{\prime},n-1)$}\mathrel{\star}{\mathsf{r}=n-1}\end{array}~\right\}}\\
\quad\!\!\text{{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}// Forward consequence: Forget $\mathsf{x}$ and fold $\mathsf{list}(x,n)$:}}\\
\quad\!\!\text{{\color[rgb]{1,0,0}// $\models\exists v,x^{\prime}.~\mathsf{x}\mapsto v,x^{\prime}\mathrel{\star}\mathsf{list}(x^{\prime},n-1)\Rightarrow\mathsf{list}(\mathsf{x},n)$.}}\\
\quad\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left\{~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\hbox{\pagecolor{slgray}$\mathsf{list}(x,n)$}\mathrel{\star}{\mathsf{r}=n-1}\end{array}~\right\}}\\
\quad\!\!\mathsf{r}\mathtt{\ :=\ }\mathsf{r}+1\\
\quad\!\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left\{~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}{\mathsf{list}(x,n)}\mathrel{\star}{\mathsf{r}=n}\end{array}~\right\}}\\
\}\\
{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\left\{~\begin{array}[]{@{} l @{}}\mathsf{list}(x,n)\mathrel{\star}{\mathsf{r}=n}\end{array}~\right\}}\end{array}
